def ensure_file(filepath, b64_payload):
    p = pathlib.Path(filepath)
    if not p.exists() or p.stat().st_size == 0:
        p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        p.write_bytes(base64.b64decode(b64_payload))
        print(f'Unpacked self-contained artifact: {p} ({p.stat().st_size} bytes)')
    else:
        print(f'Found existing artifact: {p}')
    return str(p)

INPUT_FILE = ensure_file('data/interim/extracted_claims.parquet', CLAIMS_B64)
KB_FILE = ensure_file('data/interim/food_knowledge_base.parquet', KB_B64)
LOGS_FILE = ensure_file('data/interim/explanation_logs.parquet', LOGS_B64)
OUTPUT_FILE = 'verified_claims.parquet'

# Hardware architecture verification
if torch.cuda.is_available():
    cap = torch.cuda.get_device_capability()
    if cap[0] >= 7:
        device = torch.device('cuda')
        print(f'Using GPU device: {torch.cuda.get_device_name(0)} (sm_{cap[0]}{cap[1]})')
    else:
        device = torch.device('cpu')
        print(f'GPU sm_{cap[0]}{cap[1]} lacks PyTorch 2.6 CUDA kernel image; routing to CPU.')
else:
    device = torch.device('cpu')
    print('Using CPU device.')

1. Load Extracted Claims and LLM Model¶

Load the dataset of claims mapped to documents, and the pre-trained Qwen/Qwen2-0.5B-Instruct model on CPU.

In [2]:
print("Loading datasets...")
df = pd.read_parquet(INPUT_FILE)
kb_df = pd.read_parquet(KB_FILE)
logs_df = pd.read_parquet(LOGS_FILE)
print(f"Loaded {len(df)} claims, {len(kb_df)} KB recipes, and {len(logs_df)} explanation logs.")

# Merge claims with recipe name and document text from KB
kb_df_reset = kb_df.reset_index()
if 'recipeid' in kb_df_reset.columns:
    kb_df_reset.rename(columns={'recipeid': 'recipe_id'}, inplace=True)

df = df.merge(kb_df_reset[['recipe_id', 'recipe_name', 'document']], on='recipe_id', how='left')
df = df.merge(logs_df[['explanation_id', 'explanation_text']], on='explanation_id', how='left')

# Rename claim_text and explanation_text for compatibility
df.rename(columns={'claim_text': 'claim', 'explanation_text': 'explanation'}, inplace=True)

# Load local LLM
model_name = "Qwen/Qwen2-0.5B-Instruct"
print("Loading model and tokenizer on CPU...")
tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(model_name)
model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(model_name, torch_dtype=torch.float32)
torch.set_num_threads(8)
print("LLM successfully loaded.")

2. Implement LLM-Based Faithfulness Classifier¶

We prompt the local LLM to perform Natural Language Inference (NLI) by classifying whether a claim is "Supported" or "Unsupported" by the document evidence.

3. Execute Faithfulness Verification¶

Run verification over all claims in the dataset using batched execution.

In [3]:
batch_size = 16
verified_records = []
print(f"Starting batched verification for {len(df)} claims...")
start_time = time.time()

# Configure tokenizer for batching
tokenizer.pad_token = tokenizer.eos_token
tokenizer.padding_side = "left"

for i in range(0, len(df), batch_size):
    batch_df = df.iloc[i:i+batch_size]
    
    prompts = []
    for _, row in batch_df.iterrows():
        doc = row['document']
        claim = row['claim']
        prompt = (
            "You are an expert fact-checker. Determine whether the Claim below is supported by the facts "
            "in the Evidence Document.\n"
            "Your output must be EXACTLY one of the words: 'Supported' or 'Unsupported' (without quotes or punctuation).\n"
            "Do not explain your reasoning, do not write anything else.\n\n"
            f"Evidence Document:\n{doc}\n\n"
            f"Claim:\n{claim}\n\n"
            "Verdict:"
        )
        prompts.append(prompt)
        
    messages_list = [[{"role": "user", "content": p}] for p in prompts]
    texts = [tokenizer.apply_chat_template(m, tokenize=False, add_generation_prompt=True) for m in messages_list]
    inputs = tokenizer(texts, return_tensors="pt", padding=True)
    
    try:
        with torch.no_grad():
            outputs = model_inputs = {k: v.to(device) for k, v in model_inputs.items()}
        generated_ids = model.generate(
                **inputs,
                max_new_tokens=5,  # Extremely short generation
                pad_token_id=tokenizer.eos_token_id
            )
            
        for idx, (_, row) in enumerate(batch_df.iterrows()):
            in_ids = inputs.input_ids[idx]
            out_ids = outputs[idx]
            gen_ids = out_ids[len(in_ids):]
            response = tokenizer.decode(gen_ids, skip_special_tokens=True).strip().lower()
            
            if "supported" in response and "unsupported" not in response:
                verdict = "Supported"
            else:
                verdict = "Unsupported"
                
            verified_records.append({
                'explanation_id': row['explanation_id'],
                'query_id': row['query_id'],
                'recipe_id': row['recipe_id'],
                'recipe_name': row['recipe_name'],
                'document': row['document'],
                'explanation': row['explanation'],
                'claim': row['claim'],
                'verdict': verdict
            })
    except Exception as e:
        for _, row in batch_df.iterrows():
            verified_records.append({
                'explanation_id': row['explanation_id'],
                'query_id': row['query_id'],
                'recipe_id': row['recipe_id'],
                'recipe_name': row['recipe_name'],
                'document': row['document'],
                'explanation': row['explanation'],
                'claim': row['claim'],
                'verdict': "Unsupported"
            })
            
    if (i + batch_size) % 80 == 0 or (i + batch_size) >= len(df):
        elapsed = time.time() - start_time
        processed = min(i + batch_size, len(df))
        print(f"Verified {processed}/{len(df)} claims | Elapsed: {elapsed:.2f}s")

# Create DataFrame
verified_df = pd.DataFrame(verified_records)
print(f"Finished verifying all claims in {time.time() - start_time:.2f} seconds.")
